Press releases:
  Earth [P00001] at AU 1, ocean planet [ocean]: NPC [1] — Caste Prime closes Wake the crashed ship.
    NPC records Caste Prime's fulfillment of CT0122 at Dusty field on Earth [P00001] at AU 1, ocean
    planet [ocean].
Bank report:
  Bank account balance: 8090.
  Credit line maximum: 10000.
  Credit rate: 20%, Deposit rate: 4.5%.

Technology reports:
+ advanced hull construction [ahlcns]: Recovered alien hull geometry. Automated internals need no
  crew and no life support. Unlocks TRAIN SKILL: ark helm [arkplt].
  - alien vessel hull [alnhul]: Crashed alien hull. Automated internals need no crew and no life
    support.
  - ark helm [arkplt]: Helm and formation control for generation-ship scale hulls. Training: 10
    weeks. Issue TRAIN SKILL on a person once your faction has discovered this skill.
+ automated fabrication [autfab]: An unmanned alien fabrication plant. It has no crew requirement.
  - advanced robo-factory [robofc]: Unmanned alien fabrication plant. No crew and no life support.
+ automated command systems [autctl]: A crewless command core that can wake nested alien systems.
  - automated command module [autcmd]: Crewless alien command core.
+ automated propulsion [autprp]: A crewless reaction drive sized for an alien hull.
  - automated propulsion module [autdrv]: Crewless alien drive. No life support.
+ unmanned helium plant [he3unc]: An unmanned helium-3 fusion plant with no crew stations.
  - automated helium powerplant [he3aut]: Unmanned helium-3 fusion plant. No crew.
+ small scale transportation [strans]: Transport over interstellar distances and storage of freight
  poses logistics problems.
  - small cargo bay [cargob]: The cargo bays may hold a wide variety of cargo for bulk
    transportations.
+ drone hangar construction [drnhng]: A hangar that stores and launches a squad of fighter drones.
  - fighter drone bay [drnbay]: Hangar for a squad of crewless fighter drones.
+ alien fighter construction [alnfgh]: A crewless alien fighter. It needs no life support.
  - alien fighter drone [alndrn]: Crewless alien fighter. No life support. Slow space move at
    shuttle speed; launches from a fighter drone bay.
